<section class="inner-hero" style="background-image:url('assets/images/CX-1024x1024.jpg')"><div class="container"><p><a href="index.html">Home</a> / About Our Academy</p><h1>About Our Academy</h1></div></section><main class="container ftco-section inner-main">
<div class="academy-about">
<section class="about-intro-grid"><div><p class="overline">Discover Skillpath</p><h2>A place to learn.<br>A foundation to flourish.</h2><p class="about-lead">From the first steps of childhood to the growing independence of secondary school, we nurture knowledge, character and practical skills.</p><p>Skillpath International Academy is a comprehensive school in Karu, FCT, Abuja, offering crèche, nursery, primary and secondary education. Every child brings unique abilities, interests and possibilities. Our purpose is to help them discover those strengths and develop the confidence to use them.</p><a class="btn btn-primary" href="admissions.html">Join Our Academy</a> <a class="btn btn-outline-primary" href="contact.html">Arrange a Visit</a></div><figure><img src="assets/images/CX-1024x1024.jpg" alt="Skillpath International Academy school community"><figcaption>Creativity and Excellence</figcaption></figure></section>
<section class="about-purpose-grid" aria-label="Our purpose"><article><span class="about-symbol" aria-hidden="true">◎</span><p class="overline">Our Vision</p><h3>Skills for a confident future</h3><p>To build skills that prepare children for success and excellence, developing responsibility, respect for others and the confidence to take on the world.</p></article><article><span class="about-symbol" aria-hidden="true">↗</span><p class="overline">Our Mission</p><h3>Learning that lasts a lifetime</h3><p>To provide high-quality education and childcare in a safe, respectful and inclusive environment that builds a foundation for lifelong learning.</p></article><article><span class="about-symbol" aria-hidden="true">✦</span><p class="overline">Our Motto</p><h3>Creativity and Excellence</h3><p>We encourage children to explore ideas and express their talents, while developing the preparation, perseverance and effort that underpin achievement.</p></article></section>
<section class="about-learning"><p class="overline">One academy · Four stages of growth</p><h2>Learning together, growing at every stage</h2><div class="about-stage-grid"><a href="academics/creche.html"><span class="stage-number">01</span><h3>Crèche</h3><p>Attentive care in a reassuring environment where our youngest children begin to explore, interact and develop.</p><span class="stage-link">Explore this section →</span></a><a href="academics/nursery.html"><span class="stage-number">02</span><h3>Nursery School</h3><p>A joyful foundation in communication, creativity and early learning, with room to play, discover and build confidence.</p><span class="stage-link">Explore this section →</span></a><a href="academics/primary.html"><span class="stage-number">03</span><h3>Primary School</h3><p>Purposeful learning that strengthens knowledge, positive habits, teamwork and practical skills.</p><span class="stage-link">Explore this section →</span></a><a href="academics/secondary.html"><span class="stage-number">04</span><h3>Secondary School</h3><p>Developing independent learners through academic study, critical thinking, technological awareness and responsible leadership.</p><span class="stage-link">Explore this section →</span></a></div></section>
<section class="about-whole-child"><div><p class="overline">Our educational philosophy</p><h2>Educating the whole child</h2><p>Education should help children recognise their abilities, develop their strengths and learn to overcome difficulties. We support growth academically, morally, spiritually, socially and physically.</p><p>Our teachers encourage questions, discussion, practical exploration and problem-solving. Purposeful teaching, interactive feedback and structured support help learners make progress at their own stage of development.</p><p>We uphold discipline, high moral standards and reverence for God, encouraging pupils and students to respect themselves and others and use their abilities for good.</p></div><div class="about-skills-grid"><article><span class="about-symbol" aria-hidden="true">✧</span><h3>Creativity &amp; Discovery</h3><p>Music, art, crafts, science projects and practical exploration.</p></article><article><span class="about-symbol" aria-hidden="true">⌘</span><h3>Technology &amp; Enterprise</h3><p>Connecting classroom learning with useful skills and everyday problem-solving.</p></article><article><span class="about-symbol" aria-hidden="true">⚑</span><h3>Leadership &amp; Service</h3><p>Building confidence, teamwork, compassion and responsibility to others.</p></article><article><span class="about-symbol" aria-hidden="true">◉</span><h3>Sports &amp; Wellbeing</h3><p>Developing perseverance, physical confidence and respectful competition.</p></article></div></section>
<section class="about-values"><p class="overline">The values we live by</p><h2>Character at the heart of learning</h2><div><article><span aria-hidden="true">✓</span><h3>Respect</h3><p>Valuing ourselves and every person in our community.</p></article><article><span aria-hidden="true">✓</span><h3>Integrity</h3><p>Doing what is right and taking responsibility for our actions.</p></article><article><span aria-hidden="true">✓</span><h3>Kindness</h3><p>Showing care, compassion and consideration for others.</p></article><article><span aria-hidden="true">✓</span><h3>Excellence</h3><p>Preparing well, working diligently and continually improving.</p></article><article><span aria-hidden="true">✓</span><h3>Creativity</h3><p>Exploring ideas, discovering talents and finding thoughtful solutions.</p></article><article><span aria-hidden="true">✓</span><h3>Service</h3><p>Using our abilities to contribute positively to society.</p></article></div></section>
<section class="about-visit"><div><p class="overline">A partnership with families</p><h2>Let’s bring out the best in your child.</h2><p>We welcome families who share our commitment to learning, character and personal growth. Contact the school to discuss your child’s needs or arrange a visit.</p><p>Plot 21, Supreme Court Quarters, Karu, FCT, Abuja.</p></div><a class="btn btn-secondary" href="contact.html">Contact Our School →</a></section>

<section class="inner-hero" style="background-image:url('assets/images/CX-1024x1024.jpg')"><div class="container"><p><a href="index.html">Home</a> / Admissions</p><h1>Admissions</h1></div></section><main class="container ftco-section inner-main">
<div class="row"><div class="col-lg-8"><p class="overline">Join our school community</p><h2>A New Chapter Begins at Skillpath</h2><p>We welcome enquiries for crèche, nursery, primary and secondary school. Our admissions team will guide you through the appropriate entry class and the next steps.</p><h3>Admission Process</h3><ol class="admission-steps"><li>Choose the school section and class for your child.</li><li>Complete the online application with a passport photograph and parent or guardian contact information.</li><li>Keep your application number and print your photo slip.</li><li>Receive exam and interview instructions from the admissions officer by email and phone.</li><li>Check your admission status through the applicant portal.</li><li>After admission, log in through the student portal and change your initial password.</li></ol><h3>What to Prepare</h3><p>Provide the child’s name, date of birth, passport photograph, intended class, previous school information and guardian contact details. The admissions officer will confirm any supporting documents and assessment requirements for your section.</p><a class="btn btn-primary" href="admissions/apply.html">Apply for Admission</a></div><aside class="col-lg-4 aside-box"><h3>Admissions</h3><a class="aside-link" href="admissions/requirements.html">Requirements</a><a class="aside-link" href="admissions/apply.html">Apply</a><a class="aside-link" href="portalfed0.html">Check Status</a><a class="aside-link" href="cbt.html">CBT Exams</a></aside></div>

<section class="inner-hero" style="background-image:url('assets/images/CX-1024x1024.jpg')"><div class="container"><p><a href="index.html">Home</a> / Crèche to Secondary School</p><h1>Crèche to Secondary School</h1></div></section><main class="container ftco-section inner-main">
<p class="intro">From the earliest experiences of care to the growing independence of secondary school, our purpose remains the same: helping children learn, discover, achieve and contribute.</p><div class="row"><div class="col-md-6"><article class="academic-card"><img src="assets/images/ZA-1536x1448.jpg" alt="Crèche"><div class="p-4"><h2>Crèche</h2><p>Our crèche reflects our commitment to providing childcare in a safe, caring and respectful environment. We recognise the importance of the early years and the trust parents place in those who care for their children.</p><p>Our aim is to create a reassuring setting where young children receive attentive care and support as they begin to explore, interact and develop.</p><a class="btn btn-primary" href="admissions.html">Admission Information</a></div></article></div><div class="col-md-6"><article class="academic-card"><img src="assets/images/Pre-1536x1024.jpg" alt="Nursery School"><div class="p-4"><h2>Nursery School</h2><p>Our nursery section focuses on building a positive foundation for learning. We encourage curiosity, creativity, communication and friendly interaction, helping children develop confidence as they become familiar with school life.</p><p>At this stage, care and learning work together. We seek to make children feel welcomed, valued and encouraged, while helping them develop the habits of listening, participating, sharing and respecting others.</p><a class="btn btn-primary" href="admissions.html">Admission Information</a></div></article></div><div class="col-md-6"><article class="academic-card"><img src="assets/images/SA-1536x968.jpg" alt="Primary School"><div class="p-4"><h2>Primary School</h2><p>Our primary section builds the academic and personal foundations children need for continued educational progress. We encourage pupils to ask questions, think carefully, participate actively and take pride in their work.</p><p>Alongside classroom learning, pupils have opportunities to develop creativity, practical skills, teamwork and confidence. Our approach helps them understand that learning involves both acquiring knowledge and applying it responsibly.</p><p>We also place emphasis on kindness, discipline, honesty and respect, helping pupils develop positive habits that support their education and relationships.</p><a class="btn btn-primary" href="admissions.html">Admission Information</a></div></article></div><div class="col-md-6"><article class="academic-card"><img src="assets/images/ww1-scaled.jpg" alt="Secondary School"><div class="p-4"><h2>Secondary School</h2><p>Our secondary section extends the academy’s commitment to academic achievement, character development and practical learning into the adolescent years.</p><p>We aim to help students become increasingly independent, disciplined and purposeful in their studies. Through academic work and broader school activities, we encourage critical thinking, communication, problem-solving and responsible decision-making.</p><p>Our educational philosophy recognises that secondary school is an important period for discovering interests and developing future ambitions. We therefore emphasise creativity, technological awareness, entrepreneurial thinking, leadership and service alongside academic learning.</p><p>We want our secondary school students to grow into young people who can express their ideas clearly, work constructively with others and approach further education and future responsibilities with confidence.</p><a class="btn btn-primary" href="admissions.html">Admission Information</a></div></article></div></div>

==> ./assets/site.js <==
'use strict';
document.querySelector('[data-menu]')?.addEventListener('click',function(){const menu=document.querySelector('.school-nav');menu.classList.toggle('opened');this.setAttribute('aria-expanded',String(menu.classList.contains('opened')));});
const slides=[...document.querySelectorAll('.slides .slider-item')];let slide=0;function show(n){slide=n;slides.forEach((el,i)=>el.classList.toggle('active',i===n));document.querySelectorAll('[data-slide]').forEach((el,i)=>el.classList.toggle('selected',i===n));}if(slides.length){setInterval(()=>show((slide+1)%slides.length),6500);document.querySelectorAll('[data-slide]').forEach(el=>el.addEventListener('click',()=>show(Number(el.dataset.slide))));}
document.querySelectorAll('[data-gallery]').forEach(el=>el.addEventListener('click',()=>{
 const items=[...document.querySelectorAll('[data-gallery]')].filter(item=>!item.hidden);let index=items.indexOf(el);
 const modal=document.createElement('div');modal.className='gallery-modal';modal.setAttribute('role','dialog');modal.setAttribute('aria-modal','true');modal.setAttribute('aria-label','School gallery');
 const img=document.createElement('img'),caption=document.createElement('p'),close=document.createElement('button'),prev=document.createElement('button'),next=document.createElement('button');caption.className='gallery-caption';close.textContent='×';close.setAttribute('aria-label','Close photograph');prev.textContent='Previous';next.textContent='Next';prev.className='gallery-prev';next.className='gallery-next';
 const show=n=>{index=(n+items.length)%items.length;img.src=items[index].dataset.gallery;img.alt=items[index].dataset.title||'School photograph';caption.textContent=img.alt+' · '+(index+1)+' / '+items.length;};
 const dismiss=()=>{modal.remove();document.removeEventListener('keydown',key);el.focus();};
 const key=e=>{if(e.key==='Escape')dismiss();if(e.key==='ArrowRight')show(index+1);if(e.key==='ArrowLeft')show(index-1);if(e.key==='Tab'){const focusable=[close,prev,next];const active=focusable.indexOf(document.activeElement);e.preventDefault();focusable[(active+(e.shiftKey?-1:1)+focusable.length)%focusable.length].focus();}};
 close.onclick=dismiss;prev.onclick=()=>show(index-1);next.onclick=()=>show(index+1);modal.onclick=e=>{if(e.target===modal)dismiss();};modal.append(img,caption,close,prev,next);document.body.append(modal);document.addEventListener('keydown',key);show(index);close.focus();
}));
document.querySelectorAll('[data-filter]').forEach(btn=>btn.addEventListener('click',()=>{document.querySelectorAll('[data-filter]').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',b===btn));document.querySelectorAll('[data-category]').forEach(card=>card.hidden=btn.dataset.filter!=='All'&&card.dataset.category!==btn.dataset.filter);}));
document.querySelectorAll('[data-print]').forEach(el=>el.addEventListener('click',()=>window.print()));

// Reload class enrolments before choosing an individual result.
document.querySelectorAll('.results-filter select[name=class]').forEach(select=>select.addEventListener('change',()=>{const form=select.form;const student=form.querySelector('[name=student]');if(student)student.value='0';form.requestSubmit();}));

<section class="inner-hero" style="background-image:url('../assets/images/CX-1024x1024.jpg')"><div class="container"><p><a href="../index.html">Home</a> / Admission Requirements</p><h1>Admission Requirements</h1></div></section><main class="container ftco-section inner-main">
<div class="row"><div class="col-lg-8"><p class="overline">Join our school community</p><h2>A New Chapter Begins at Skillpath</h2><p>We welcome enquiries for crèche, nursery, primary and secondary school. Our admissions team will guide you through the appropriate entry class and the next steps.</p><h3>Admission Process</h3><ol class="admission-steps"><li>Choose the school section and class for your child.</li><li>Complete the online application with a passport photograph and parent or guardian contact information.</li><li>Keep your application number and print your photo slip.</li><li>Receive exam and interview instructions from the admissions officer by email and phone.</li><li>Check your admission status through the applicant portal.</li><li>After admission, log in through the student portal and change your initial password.</li></ol><h3>What to Prepare</h3><p>Provide the child’s name, date of birth, passport photograph, intended class, previous school information and guardian contact details. The admissions officer will confirm any supporting documents and assessment requirements for your section.</p><a class="btn btn-primary" href="apply.html">Apply for Admission</a></div><aside class="col-lg-4 aside-box"><h3>Admissions</h3><a class="aside-link" href="requirements.html">Requirements</a><a class="aside-link" href="apply.html">Apply</a><a class="aside-link" href="../portalfed0.html">Check Status</a><a class="aside-link" href="../cbt.html">CBT Exams</a></aside></div>
